# D-007 · CEIR — THE MASTER TICK TRACKER

> **THE single live tracker for all active work** (user-directed 2026-08-07: CEIR is the master spine; strict band
> order; RAH-1/2 parallel; everything else paused). One row per sub-slice; first column is the tick:
> `✅` done · `◧` in progress · `⬜` todo · `◀ NEXT` marks the front. **A row ticks ✅ ONLY at its GATE** — never
> because code or a "head" exists (the D-007 house rule).
>
> ### The three documents, and which one wins
> | Document | Role | When you read it |
> |---|---|---|
> | **Mission constitution** — [`../research/2026-08-07-ceir-universal-programming-master-roadmap.md`](../research/2026-08-07-ceir-universal-programming-master-roadmap.md) (§0–§185) | **THE LAW.** The full architecture, every contract, every rule. | Read the §§ a row cites **before implementing that row.** |
> | **This tracker** | THE INDEX + tick state. Summarizes each slice and cites its §§; never restates the law. | To see what's next and what's done. |
> | **Per-band design spec** — `docs/design/ceir-<band>-<slug>.md` (written at band OPEN) | The reuse audit (what already exists, file:line) + sequenced increments + per-increment gate. The house convention (see `docs/design/README.md`). | When you were told "read the slice and implement it." |
> | **Landed history** — [`D-007-gpu-program-system.md`](D-007-gpu-program-system.md) | The CKIR/RAF/RET ledger + the pre-CEIR post-RAF plan. Its live-tracking role **MOVED here at CEIR-0f (2026-08-08)** — that doc's master table is now historical. | For "how did we get here." |
>
> **Citation convention:** `§NN` = section NN of the mission constitution. Its headings are `# NN. TITLE`, so
> searching `# 39.` jumps to §39. A row's `→ §§` list is the *complete* set of sections that define its contract.

# Architecture in one screen — READ THIS BEFORE THE ROWS

**The one sentence (§1 north star).** Every reusable algorithm expressible from capabilities Cerid *already*
understands must be a **versioned, inspectable, serializable, hot-reloadable program asset** — whether it does
rendering, GPU compute, ray tracing, ML/AI, scientific computing, media, geometry, UI, physics, animation, audio,
or application logic. New native C++ is required **only** to introduce a genuinely new *host capability / OS
integration / device provider / hardware primitive* — never merely because someone invented a new *algorithm* from
existing capabilities.

**The mantra (§1).**
```
ALGORITHMS ARE PROGRAM ASSETS.   CAPABILITIES ARE NATIVE PRIMITIVES.
COMPILERS CHOOSE LOWERINGS.      BACKENDS EXECUTE.
```

**The stack (§3) — five layers, one-way lowering.**
```
AUTHORING   text · CR-D007 visual · TOML/domain frontends · C++ builder · importers      §9 §11 §121 §124
      │     (all projections emit the SAME canonical IR — there is no privileged path)     §121 §166
      ▼
CHIR        Cerid High-level IR — future language layer (modules, generics, ADTs,          §4  (design-only now;
      │     closures, traits, ownership, async/state/events). Lowers/erases into CEIR.          implemented CEIR-32)
      ▼
CEIR        Cerid Execution IR — typed SSA + graph/CFG/structured regions; resources,       §2 §12 §13
      │     effects, capabilities, evaluation domains; ~30 open-world dialects (§33–§68).
      ├───────────────────────────┐
      ▼                           ▼
CKIR (per-invocation kernel math,  EXECUTION PROVIDERS (CPU/GPU/NPU/media/remote —          §69 §85
 the shipped shader/kernel IR)      native graph/program APIs; advertise ops/costs)
      └───────────────┬───────────┘
                      ▼
COMPILED EXECUTION PLAN  →  Vulkan · D3D12 · CUDA · HIP · Metal · WebGPU · CPU/SIMD/jobs     §144 §153
                            · codecs · NPU · remote nodes
```

**The three IRs, crisply.**
- **CKIR** *(exists, 100% of the engine, bit-exact)* — **what one GPU invocation computes.** Per-thread shader/
  kernel math. Unchanged by this detour; CEIR references CKIR programs by asset identity and may *generate* CKIR
  during lowering (§85 §86).
- **CEIR** *(this detour)* — **what work exists, what data/resources flow, what controls execution, where work can
  run, how work generates more work, how resources synchronize, and which lower program implements each op** (§2).
- **CHIR** *(design-only now, §4)* — **source-language semantics** (generics, ADTs, closures, ownership, reflection)
  that must NOT be baked into an execution IR. Lowers/erases into CEIR. Implemented at CEIR-32, after the CEIR
  corpus exists to teach the language its ergonomics.

**Why it is NOT a giant enum (§6 §7).** Completeness comes from **open-world extensibility**, not a finite opcode
list. CEIR is MLIR-shaped: `Operation` + typed operands/results + attributes + **regions** + **dialects**
(registered op sets) + **interfaces/traits** — analyses dispatch on interfaces, never `switch(op.kind)`. A new
algorithm is a new *asset*; a new abstraction is a CEIR function or a custom op that *lowers* to existing ops
(§101 Level A/B); only a genuinely new hardware/host primitive is native C++ (§100, §101 Level C). ⛔ A giant
`enum CeirOp { Draw, Dispatch, ForwardPlus, Deferred, Nanite, Lumen … }` is the FORBIDDEN destination — it repeats
the exact `FramePassKind` combinatorial mistake RAF-12 just deleted.

**What stays native FOREVER (§177):** backend API calls, OS/window, filesystem/network impl, device enumeration,
swapchain acquire/present, vendor compiler invocation, hardware codec/NPU providers, native sensors, the job-system
impl, the compiler/runtime itself. **What must NOT stay native-only (§178):** renderer architectures, culling,
lighting, post-processing, GI, RT workflows, compute chains, numerical workflows, tensor/ML graphs, media chains,
UI effect graphs, geometry pipelines.

**Visual = a projection, not a runtime (§9 §10 §166).** Visual and textual authoring are two lenses on ONE typed
semantic program. Graph coordinates/edges are UI; canonical semantics are operations/values/regions/symbols. A
user function with a typed interface automatically becomes a node (§11). Lenses: dataflow · structured control-flow
· frame/workflow · state-machine · reactive/event · timeline (§9).

**House rules on EVERY row** (not repeated per row): crd containers only (`IAllocator` ctor arg, no std) ·
serialization is FIELD-BY-FIELD, never raw-POD memcpy (the padding/content-hash scar) · ASCII test names · run
`ctest`, never the bare binary · tidy per touched file · GPU gates = both backends + `crd::gpu::ValidationCapture` ·
parallel results carry the `{1..16}` determinism moat · Allman / 4-space / 120-col.

**Naming note:** the CEIR-1 C++ type names are **FINALIZED by ADR-0109 §5** (`crd::ceir::{Context, Module,
Operation, Value, Block, Region, SymbolTable, Type, Dialect, OpId, SourceLoc, KernelRef, IExecutionProvider,
ModuleBuilder}`) — no longer a working proposal. Later-band op/dialect names remain proposals until their band opens.

**Row-refinement rule:** CEIR-0…16 carry full sub-slice rows now; CEIR-17…35 carry one gated row each and split
into sub-slices at band OPEN, sized by CEIR-0z's estimates (far-band detail is not invented before the inventory).

**Pause policy (§176):** until CEIR-16z, allowed work = bug fixes · unsafe half-migration completion · CKIR fixes ·
tests · docs · RAH-1/2 · CEIR slices. Parked work is tabled at the bottom so nothing is silently dropped.

## CEIR-1 — Core IR substrate — ✅ CLOSED 2026-08-08 (1a–1z) → §175 band 1 · core: §12 §7

> **BAND 1 CLOSED.** The full host-only IR substrate: identity + the in-arena def-use graph (1a) · SymbolTable +
> `ceir.func` (1b) · interned typed attributes + source map (1c) · open-world dialect registry + traits/interfaces +
> verifier (1d) · deterministic textual printer + parser (1e) · FourCC-chunked binary serialization (1f) ·
> `ModuleBuilder` fluent API (1g) · the round-trip fuzz + malformed corpus + stable-hash harness (1h) · the
> hello-world band gate (1z). `tests/ceir` **49/49** (from 7 at 1a) + a 7/7 `crd::memory::GrowableLinearAllocator`
> gate. Every slice gated on **win-debug + win-asan + linux-gcc-debug + linux-gcc-asan + LLVM-20 tidy** +
> `crd-ceir-invariants` (I3/I5/I6). Headline finding: the **1h fuzz caught two real OOM crashes** in loaders that had
> already passed four slices of unit gates. **NEXT: user commits the batch → GitHub CI green → CEIR-2.**

**Band contract.** New module `engine/ceir` — the in-memory IR + its two serial forms. Working shape (finalized in
0c): `crd::ceir::Context` (owns dialect registry + interned types/attrs/strings), `Module`, `Op` (interned
`(dialect,name)` id + `ValueId` operand/result spans + attr map + region list, **arena-allocated** per §153),
`Value` (typed, def-use chain), `Block`, `Region{Graph|SsaCfg}` (§13), `SymbolTable`. Textual form is MLIR-shaped
(`%v = dialect.op(%a,%b) {attr=…} : type`), printed DETERMINISTICALLY. Binary form is FourCC-chunked like CRDR,
versioned from byte one (§104), field-by-field (⛔ padding scar). **Reuse:** `crd::containers` + TLSF/arena; FNV-1a
interning; the CRDR chunk pattern. **Gate: typed hello-world round-trips text/binary/builder (§167).**

|---|---|---|---|

## CEIR-2 — Schema/ODS-like op-definition generator — ✅ CLOSED (2a–2z) 2026-08-08 → §8

**Band contract (§8).** Ops are DEFINED in TOML (authoring-text house rule), generated to C++ — never hand-kept in
parallel. `*.ceirop.toml` per dialect under `engine/ceir/ops/`; generator `tools/ceir_opgen/ceir_opgen.py` (checked
in, output committed — the `gen_fft_batched.py` precedent) emits: typed op wrapper + builder + verifier scaffold +
reflection record + CR-D007 node metadata + CLI/MCP schema + doc stub + test skeleton. Schema fields per §8: name ·
dialect · version · operands(+variadic) · results · attrs · regions · traits · effects · domain legality ·
capability requirements · type/shape inference · fold · serialization schema · editor hints · docs · deprecation.
**Gate: a new pure op is added with no central enum/switch edits (proves §7).**

high-level types, erased at the kernel/raw boundary** (§17 — reuse `crd::units`, do NOT reinvent); ownership/view
qualifiers (§19). **CHIR headroom NOW:** generic params + constraint records + interface/trait types + callable
types exist in the IR before any frontend emits them (append-at-END, type-system edition). **Gate: invalid unit/
shape/view combinations are rejected.**

the non-escaping borrow and the escaping *owned* value are both correctly ignored — the type-directed contract); **(4) generic-constraint violation** — `substitute`→`SubstResult.failed_param`/`failed_trait` (exact `(T, Ord)`, + an accept control after `register_conformance`). ⭐ **Scope boundary (advisor, honest):** 3z ships pointing **predicates** + the one `find_borrowed_escape` module walk (the borrowed-view escape is a program property whose inputs — typed values, regions, uses — exist NOW); it is **NOT** a module-level type verifier — op-level wiring of (1)/(2)/(4) onto typed operands composes at **CEIR-4** (no typed-operand producer op exists yet — the 3e "predicate-now" precedent). **Pointing upgrade to the 3f surface:** `value_escapes_region` (bool) gained the companion `first_escaping_use(Value*,Region*)→const Operation*` (a bool can't point; a SourceLoc-bearing op can); `value_escapes_region` now delegates to it (no API break). `find_borrowed_escape` is **first-offender** (deterministic pre-order = the printer's SSA order), **structural not dominance-aware** (walks uses; the CFG/dominance verifier is CEIR-5b), **direct-use + type-directed** (a leak through an untyped intermediate is caught only if the intermediate itself carries the borrowed type). ## CEIR-4 — Effect + determinism model — ✅ CLOSED (4a–4z) → §26 §27 §15

**Band contract.** Every effectful op declares semantic effects (§26 families) optionally carrying resource/range
identity — the frame graph's proven read/write/lifetime discipline (WAR-needs-lifetime, RMW-not-RWM scars) promoted
to first-class IR. Determinism classes (§27) **align 1:1 with the ADR-0098 T1/T2/T3 tiers CKIR already certifies** —
ONE vocabulary. Evaluation domains (§15) + real-time classes (§32) as metadata with legality checking. Numerical
semantics (§28) attach here. **Gate: compiler distinguishes reorderable vs ordered ops correctly.**

| ✔ | Slice | Deliverable | → §§ |
|---|---|---|---|
| ✅ | CEIR-4a | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-09.** **The §26 effect vocabulary + typed effect records, declared via the 2a schema.** `effect.hpp`: **`EffectFamily`** (all **27** §26 families verbatim, `u8`, ⛔ APPEND-AT-END — a `static_assert` pins `Debug==26` in lockstep with the generator's `EFFECT_FAMILIES`; NO subsetting per NO-FOLLOW, `MemoryReadWrite` stays a family) + **`EffectRecord`** POD `{EffectFamily family; EffectTarget target(None/Operand/Result); u32 index; u32 range_mask}` (a trivially-copyable POD, no StringView, so generated `constexpr` arrays + arena copies are cheap; `range_mask` reuses the **3c `ViewRange`** bitmask, 0=whole resource). Declaration is **KIND-LEVEL** (an effect names an operand/result POSITION; per-instance SSA resolution is CEIR-4d; instance-*dependent* effects — a call's callee-derived set — get an `EffectsFn` hook at CEIR-5, NOT built now [✅ BUILT at CEIR-5c: `OpInfo::effects_fn` + `Context::effective_effects`]). ⭐ **Attach point = B1 (advisor):** effects live on **`OpInfo`** (beside traits+verify), passed to `register_op(op, traits, verify, effects={})` which **arena-COPIES** them (the caller's span need not outlive the call — the alloc-outlives-borrowers rule), queried via **`Context::op_effects(OpId)`** — NOT a CEIR-1d `InterfaceId` and NOT reflection-only; the "effect *interface*" IS `op_effects` over `OpInfo` (the row's word is not a `1d` interface). ⛔ **EMPTY≠UNKNOWN contract** (header + tested): `op_effects` empty means "registered + provably effect-free" ONLY when `op_info(kind)!=nullptr`; an UNREGISTERED kind (§6.11 unknown dialect) is MAXIMALLY effectful, never reorderable. **⛔ func.call landmine (advisor):** a registered op that forgets to declare reads as *provably* effect-free — `func.call` defaulted so → now declares a conservative `ExternalCall` barrier (memory `feedback_registered_default_empty_reads_as_provably_none`). **Pure ⇒ zero effects** (§26) at BOTH live arms — the generator (pointing cook error) + a `register_op` assert (+ a hand-path `family≤Debug && target≤Result` bound). **2a schema:** TOML `effects` accepts a bare family string OR an inline table `{family, operand|result, range=[...]}` — the generator validates the family vocabulary + operand/result index bounds + range names (pointing errors), emits typed `EffectRecord` arrays (before `register_%s_ops` so both it and the `OpSchema` table reuse them), and `OpSchema.effects` upgrades `StringView[]→EffectRecord[]` (the "strings until CEIR-4" scaffold retired; `.ops.json` effects went `string[]→object[]` under schema_version 1 deliberately — a scaffold field, no external consumers). Effects are **registration-time data, NOT serialized** — no binary surface touched, no version bump. `test.ceirop.toml` exercises BOTH forms (full-surface reference). ## CEIR-5 — Structured control flow + functions — ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-09** (5a ✅ 5b ✅ 5c ✅ 5d ✅ 5z ✅) → §13 §14 §20

**Accumulated landings (placed at 4-band close, advisor):** the `EffectsFn` hook (func.call's callee-derived effects) → **CEIR-5c** ✅ **DONE** (`OpInfo::effects_fn` + `Context::effective_effects`, consumed by the hazard `table` overloads + `find_domain_violation`, closing the 4c "ExternalCall stays legal-for-now" gap); cross-block hazards → **5b at earliest, honestly re-deferred to CEIR-15d** (5b builds dominance/terminator *verification*, not CFG-reachability hazard analysis); nested-region-relax (may a child region relax its parent's realtime class) → ~~**5d/5z** (needs execution semantics — an executor that RUNS regions gives relax its meaning)~~ **[✅ RE-ROUTED at 5z close (struck in place): the CEIR-5z reference executor is a SEMANTIC executor — it never consults `region_exec` (a region tag is a scheduler/domain-legality concern, not a value-semantics one), so "an executor that runs regions" INFORMS but does NOT decide relax. Nested-region-relax is a SCHEDULER concern → CEIR-15 (the frame/schedule band), where region execution constraints actually bind.]**


**Band contract.** `ceir.core` structured ops — `if`/`switch`/`for`/`foreach`/`while`/`match`/`scope` as
region-carrying ops (§13 §14); SSACFG blocks+branches beneath them; calls + bounded-recursion attrs; explicit
state: `state<T>`/`history<T>`/`delay<T>` (§20) — graph cycles ONLY through state/delay (verifier rule). Plus the
first **reference executor** skeleton (host, slow, correct — §118) running core+func+state. **Gate: a nontrivial
program executes in the reference host runtime.**

| ✅ | CEIR-5a | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-09** (landed across a checkpoint + a continuation; ONE commit supersedes the checkpoint proposal). ⭐ **DONE:** (1) **Generator region-SIGNATURE schema evolution** — a `regions` list entry may declare `{kind, args=[{name, type_hint}], variadic}`; `type_hint` is a DOC string (NO TOML type grammar — the 3a lesson; real arg types are construction-time), the arg COUNT is the structural contract, `variadic` only on the last entry (validation + verifier emission + 4 py tests). (2) The **`ceir.core` dialect** via 2a schemas: **`scope`** (1 region), **`if`** (cond + 2 regions), **`for`**/**`foreach`** (1 region with a typed entry-block arg — induction var / element), **`yield`** (variadic-operand Terminator). ⛔ Every core op is **BitExact** (a control-flow CONSTRUCT is deterministic) and declares **ZERO effects** (a structured op is not itself effectful — its effects are its CONTENTS', which the CEIR-4d per-block hazard walk already sees). (3) **`Context::fold_constant_if`** — the constant-condition `if` fold (partial-eval seed): a constant `arith.const` cond splices the TAKEN region's single block into the parent before the `if` and erases the `if`. ⛔ **BAILS (no mutation)** on: not-a-`core.if`; non-constant cond; multi-block region; missing `core.yield` terminator; a `region_exec`-tagged `if` (CEIR-4c — inlining would delete the domain constraint); AND ⛔ **any `region_exec`-tagged op INSIDE the taken block** (the 2nd band-4-metadata-breaks-a-band-5-rewrite case — a rewrite must audit the attrs it MOVES, not only the op it deletes). ⭐ **DONE (the continuation):** (4) **Variadic RESULTS** — the generator's 3rd variadic axis (validation + variadic-aware `num_results` verifier); `scope`/`if` gain variadic results (`results = [{variadic}]`), value-producing. (5) **`while`** (2 regions: cond + body; structural now — the cond-yields-exactly-1-bool contract is CEIR-5b). (6) **`switch`/`match`** — VARIADIC case regions (a selector/scrutinee operand + N regions; STRUCTURAL only, PATTERN semantics are CHIR's). (7) **`fold_constant_if` RAUW value-forwarding** — folds the taken region (THEN *and* ELSE contents, both tested) and RAUWs the `if`'s results with the taken `yield`'s operands; a new ⛔ bail on a yield-operand-count ≠ if-result-count MISMATCH (else the RAUW would index OOB — a UAF-shaped bug). **The yield↔owner count contract is enforced at its SOLE consumer (the fold); the general region-walking verifier lands at CEIR-5b** (where the 3f `parent_op` back-link earns its keep). **FOUR design decisions a resumer inherits:** SKELETON-VERIFIES (entry-block arg count checked only WHEN a block exists — existence is 5b); STATEMENTS-first→VALUES-landed; **HOMOGENEOUS result types** (all results share one placeholder type — per-result typing joins at CEIR-6 inference, and the fold REPLACES result values with the yields regardless, so NO `create_operation` overload was needed); **MIN-ARITY builders** (a generated builder produces the minimum arity on every variadic axis — operands/results/regions; full-arity construction is `create_operation`; noted in the generated hpp banner). | ✅ | CEIR-5b | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-09.** **The §115 structure verifier.** `Context::find_structure_error(Module&) -> StructureError{op, value, kind}` — ONE top-down region walk returning the FIRST structural defect (deterministic pre-order = the printer's SSA order, the 3z first-offender convention) or `None`. `StructureErrorKind` is a **no-default `-Werror=switch`** enum (6): `UseBeforeDef`, `CaptureThroughIsolation`, `MissingTerminator`, `TerminatorNotLast`, `YieldCountMismatch`. **[⚠ CEIR-5d extended this enum +2 (`FeedbackWithoutState`, `StateDepthInvalid`) and RE-CLASSIFIED same-block forward/self references from `UseBeforeDef` to `FeedbackWithoutState` — the 5b use-before-def tests' expectations were updated in place; a genuine `UseBeforeDef` is now a LATER-block / never-defined value.]** **Three §115 checks for the current (no-branch-op) world:** **(1) dominance = per-block def-before-use + capture visibility** — a `DomScope` chain (each block frame owns its `defs`: block args, then op results appended AS the walk passes each op) models the visible SSA environment; ⛔ **isolation-aware** — the walk STOPS at an `IsolatedFromAbove` frame, so a capture that WOULD be visible without isolation but is blocked by it is `CaptureThroughIsolation`, a distinct pointing diagnostic **discriminated by re-probing visibility with isolation OFF** (vs `UseBeforeDef`). **(2) terminators by RegionKind** — an `SsaCfg` block MUST end with a `Terminator`-trait op (an EMPTY block fails too); a `Graph` region imposes none (order is data/effects). ⭐ **The 3f `parent_op` back-link EARNS ITS KEEP here:** an empty SsaCfg block has no op to blame → `MissingTerminator` points at the region's OWNER via `Region::parent_op()` (a diagnostic must point at *something* — 3z; the outermost module body is Graph so this is always non-null). `TerminatorNotLast` catches a non-last Terminator. **(3) the yield↔owner COUNT contract** — generalized from its SOLE 5a consumer (the fold) to every `core.*` region owner: the terminating `core.yield`'s operand count == the owner's result count, with the table override that `core.while`'s cond region (region 0) yields exactly **1** (the loop test). **⚠ Re-scope carried from the 5a `while` line (struck in place):** 5b enforces the cond **COUNT**; **BOOL-ness → CEIR-6** typing (core-op results are HOMOGENEOUS placeholders — a cond fed by a structured result has no checkable element type today). **FOUR conscious relaxations (advisor pre-close) a resumer must not mis-infer:** (a) the yield contract binds `core.*` owners ONLY — a foreign-dialect region owner is unconstrained (open-world / I6-consistent); ## CEIR-6 — Async/task/runtime domains — ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-09** (6a ✅ 6b ✅ 6c ✅ 6z ✅) → §30 §31 §37 §38

**Band contract.** `ceir.async` (tokens/async values/join/race/cancel, §37) + `ceir.task` (§38) with the **host job
provider lowering onto crd-jobs** — `task.parallel_for`→`crd::jobs::parallel_for` (6b) + `task.map_reduce` fixed-order reduction (6z); ~~`task.spawn`/waits→jobs
counters/fiber waits, main-thread pinning, deadline/priority attrs~~ **[6z: band 6 ships the async+parallel+map-reduce CORE; priority landed 6c (`priority_for` in the bridge), deadline → CEIR-24/29 (no crd-jobs primitive), spawn/continuation/main-thread/worker/fiber-wait/task-group + jobs-backed launch/await → CEIR-11a (§84 async-host subset) where a compiled-plan consumer needs them]**. ⛔ No second scheduler (§31): CEIR is an
authorable front-end to the existing fibers. This is where the **first execution provider** (host/jobs) is born in
minimal form (§69). **Scars:** worker_index aliasing · frame-arena exhaustion under parallel_for · workers reset on
shutdown. **Gate: a job-system parallel program is authored as CEIR.**

BAND 6 OPENS.** **`ceir.async` ops + the §116 use-once token-misuse verifier.** A NEW `ceir.async` dialect (via the 2a generator): **`launch { region } -> %token`** (structured fork — §30 structured concurrency, NOT detached spawn), **`await(%token) -> (%v…)`** (consume, get the body's yields), **`join(%t…) -> %token`** (all-of), **`race(%t…) -> %index`** (any-of; index-only — value-carrying race needs async-value `!async<T>` types → routed within §37 to CEIR-6b/6c), **`cancel(%token)`**. ⛔ **Tokens are TRAIT-typed, not TYPE-typed (advisor):** two NEW OpTrait bits — **`TokenProducer`** (`1<<6`, convention: EVERY result is a token) + **`TokenConsumer`** (`1<<7`, convention: EVERY operand is a consumed token — revisitable per the StateEdge-last-operand precedent when a mixed-operand op arrives); **`join` carries BOTH**, `race` is consumer-only (its index result is NOT a token). A producer trait can't be forgotten, whereas a `!token` TYPE would be invisible to a mistyped op (the 3z construction hole) + would drag print/parse/canonical/binary/fuzz — the `!async<T>` async-VALUE type is NAMED-deferred to the slice that needs typed results (NO-FOLLOW). **The verifier `Context::find_token_misuse(Module&) -> {value, op, kind}`** — a USE-ONCE (linear) def-use walk (§116 is the taxonomy; the MECHANISM is linear use-counting — 4d's read/write hazards cannot express "exactly once"): every token consumed **EXACTLY once** by a `TokenConsumer` operand SLOT. Kinds (no-default `-Werror=switch`): **`Unconsumed`** (0 — a leaked async op, no await XOR cancel), **`MultiplyConsumed`** (>1 SLOTS — double-await / `join(t,t)` counts as 2), **`ConsumedByNonConsumer`** (a token operand of a non-consumer op). Pre-order first-offender (use-misuses caught at the use; Unconsumed post-walk at the producer); trait-keyed = open-world (the core never name-checks async). **FOUR conscious edges, each tested:** a token consumed in BOTH `if`-branches = 2 static uses = flagged (no path-sensitivity substrate — named); a token yielded out of a region / passed to `func.call` / fed to arith = `ConsumedByNonConsumer` ⇒ **tokens are region- AND function-CONFINED, so §30's structured discipline falls out FREE**; produce-outside / consume-inside a LOOP = 1 static use = passes (dynamic multiplicity needs execution — a documented gap). ⛔ **Register-to-verify (opposite direction from 5d):** an UNregistered async module has no `TokenProducer` traits ⇒ zero tokens tracked ⇒ a leak passes VACUOUSLY (a false NEGATIVE — the perf-flag-measures-empty-frame shape); entry-reachable scoping is a later refinement). **Sequential-reference ASYNC** (the 6a IOU's SEQUENTIAL half, paid in crd-ceir): `exec::install_async_semantics` — launch runs the body at launch, the token's runtime value = a **SESSION-scoped handle** into an Interpreter yield-store (⛔ a forged/cross-session token → `ExecError::BadToken`, NOT a silent empty — the vacuous-pass shape closed at the executor), await returns the stashed yields, join concatenates, race → index 0 deterministically, cancel = consume/no-op ("real cancellation is 6c"); JOBS-BACKED parallel async → **6c** (struck in the 6a row). NEW crd-ceir surface (each with a real consumer): `Interpreter` prototype ctor + `allocator()`/scratch (the per-item clones); `invoke_region` (run a body region with block-args — the per-index runner; ⛔ the body must be SELF-CONTAINED — outer captures are `UndefinedValue`, tested + pinned in the TOML docs); `set_user`/`user()` (the provider threads its parallel context); `store_yields`/`valid_yield_handle`/`stored_yields` (the async installer). §38's other seven ops (spawn/continuation/main-thread/worker/fiber-wait/deadline-priority/task-group) ~~NAMED-deferred to **6c**~~ **[re-struck at 6c: 6c built NONE of them — of the seven, PRIORITY landed (`priority_for` map in the bridge) and DEADLINE is declined-with-reason → **CEIR-24/29** (§69 provider planning, where a deadline-aware scheduler is decided); the remaining SIX (spawn/continuation/main-thread/worker/fiber-wait/task-group) EXCEED band-6's remaining capacity — 6z is a GATE not a feature slice, and 21/26 are ML/graph-provider rows — so they are SURFACED for the user's 6z prompt to place (candidate: a 6d extension slice)]** **[RESOLVED at 6z → CEIR-11a (§84 async-host subset), where a compiled-plan consumer pulls the full §38 task vocabulary — NOT a 6d; 6z closes the band]**. ⛔ TESTS own the `crd::jobs` pool (a Catch listener init/shutdown; the provider NEVER does — the workers-reset scar). BAND-6 GATE MET — a parallel map-reduce AUTHORED as CEIR runs on the fiber pool, bit-identical across `{1..16}`.** The in-IR reduction the `task.parallel_for` docs pinned to 6z: a NEW **`task.map_reduce(%lo, %hi, %step, %init)` `map { ^m(%iv): … } combine { ^c(%acc, %elem): … } -> %result`** op (via the 2a generator) — operand order **(lower, upper, step, init)** so the shared `run_parallel_map` helper reads operands 0..2 identically for both task ops (advisor). ⭐ **The provider (`HostProvider`, crd-ceir-host) runs the MAP in PARALLEL** (`crd::jobs::parallel_for`, disjoint index-order slots — the exact 6b machinery, refactored into `run_parallel_map` shared with `parallel_for`), **then FOLDS the results SEQUENTIALLY in INDEX ORDER** (`acc = init; acc = combine(acc, out[i])` for i=0..count) — so the result is num_jobs-INDEPENDENT and **bit-identical across ALL {1..16}** even for a NON-associative combine: the map output is index-order-disjoint (split-independent) and the fold order is FIXED. ⛔ **The fold runs on ONE fresh sub-interpreter cloned from the prototype — NOT a re-entrant `invoke_region` on the mid-invoke `in`** (its frame is live — advisor landmine); a plain i64 accumulator, `set_cancel_flag` threaded so `request_cancel` also stops a long reduce; a fold-step error is attributed to the map_reduce op (index-order first). `map_reduce` has an **SSA result** (unlike statement `parallel_for`) so it COMPOSES inside `async.scope` — the gate wraps the map-reduce in `async.scope { %r = map_reduce…; yield %r }`, ⭐ **`async.scope`'s FIRST run through HostProvider** (the scope×provider composition seam 6c could only test in-core). **Pre-flight** (submit thread) unified into `check_parallel_region`, extended to BOTH regions: the map yields 1 + 1 block-arg (%iv); the combine yields 1 + **2 block-args (%acc, %elem) — arg count checked so `invoke_region`'s bind never trips `BadArity` mid-fold** (this also hardens the latent parallel_for map-arity gap); both StateEdge-free transitively (a cell would make the fold depend on the split). ⭐ **THE GATE TEST (`[host]`):** `@main() { %s = async.scope { %r = map_reduce(0, 37, 1, init=0) map{iv: iv*iv} combine{acc,elem: acc*31 + elem}; yield %r }; return %s }` — run through HostProvider for **EVERY num_jobs ∈ {1..16}** (N=**37, a PRIME ≥16** so every split is ragged — no even divide), each result **byte-pinned (`pin_values`) and asserted byte-identical to num_jobs=1 AND to an INDEPENDENT wrapping-u64 reference fold** (the combine is NON-associative `acc*31+elem` — bit-identity alone would pass 16 identical WRONGS, the bit-exact-blind scar; the reference wraps in u64 to mirror the executor's i64, else the test itself is signed-overflow UB). + a `map_reduce` pre-flight TEST_CASE (stateful combine → `ParallelBodyStateful`; wrong-arity combine → `BadArity`; combine yields≠1 → `ParallelYieldArity`) + the **empty-range identity** (`map_reduce(0,0,1,init=42) → 42`, run **BARE outside any scope** — the happy path off the scope seam; advisor) + `advertises(task.map_reduce)`. NO new `ExecError` (arg-count reuses `BadArity` — the same error `invoke_region` raises), NO exec.hpp/cpp change (the reduction lives entirely in the bridge). **DEFERRALS ROUTED (advisor ledger):** jobs-backed launch/await-ON-POOL + §38's six residual task ops (spawn/continuation/main-thread/worker/fiber-wait/task-group) → **CEIR-11a** (§84 async-host subset — inbound noted at 8a); the scope×provider seam is PAID here. Tests: `test_host_provider.cpp` +2 TEST_CASEs (the gate + map_reduce pre-flight) + a map_reduce empty-range SECTION → **`[host]` 12 cases**. **Gate:** crd-ceir-tests **209/209 ctest** (207 + 2 `[host]`; both OSes via `ctest -R ceir`) on **win-debug + win-asan + linux-gcc-debug + linux-gcc-asan** (⭐ **the {1..16} bit-identity proven ASan-clean under 4 threads on linux-gcc-asan — the money config this gate existed to reach**) + LLVM-20 tidy (2 files) + GCC `-Werror=switch` (no new switch — arg-count reuses BadArity) + `crd-ceir-opgen-{drift,validator}` (drift green post-regen: the new op) + **`crd-ceir-invariants` (crd-ceir STILL jobs-free — the whole reduction is in the BRIDGE)** + invariants. **No binary version bump.** ⭐ **BAND 6 CLOSED.** | §30 §31 |

## ⭐ THE 2026-08-09 UNIVERSALITY RE-BASELINE (user-directed; full renumber per the quest's §113)

**Why.** The universality quest (`docs/research/2026-08-09-ceir-universality-review.md` — the standing review doc;
its section numbers cited below as **U-§n**) audited the shipped substrate (bands 1–7b, 232 tests, 4 configs)
against the standard *"one coherent programmable substrate for every computational domain Cerid may target —
engine, MATLAB-class, DAW, DCC, CAD/CAM, EDA/PCB, simulation, ML, media, agents"*. Verdict: the op layer is
genuinely open-world (interned OpId, no central switch, unknown-dialect preservation — I6-gated), but **types,
attributes, effect families, effect locations, and region kinds are closed vocabularies; semantic identity is
pointer-based; there is no analysis/pass/rewrite framework, no incremental-evaluation model, no transaction model,
and capability contracts have no home.** Several of these are RECORDED debts: ADR-0109 §6 promised stable semantic
identity at CEIR-1c (only SourceLoc landed); `attr.hpp:6` promised extended attribute kinds at "CEIR-2/3" (never
landed); the 7a close flagged capability contracts ownerless and state cells instance-keyed. **CEIR-8 pays these
debts; CEIR-9 proves the payment; CEIR-10 resumes band 7 on the closed foundation.** Nothing shipped is rewritten —
every foundation slice EXTENDS the live substrate with all prior tests green (serialization/hash changes are
versioned recooks, named per slice).

**The numbering map (old → new).** Closed bands 0–7 keep their numbers (frozen ledger). Everything ⬜ renumbered:

| Old | New | | Old | New | | Old | New |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| — | **8 Foundation Closure (NEW)** | | 10 Compute+transfer | 13 | | 22 AD | 25 |
| — | **9 Universality Validation (NEW)** | | 11 Render | 14 | | 23 Optimizer-1 | 26 |
| 7c/7d/7z | **10a/10b/10z** | | 12 FrameGraph | 15 | | 24 Transform IR | 27 |
| 8 Ref executor | 11 | | 13 Executor migration | 16 | | 25 Autotune | 28 |
| 9 Resource/memory | 12 | | 14–21 | 17–24 (+3) | | 26–32 | 29–35 (+3) |

**Cadence (user verdict 2026-08-09):** foundation ADRs are advisor-approved, fully autonomous (the gold-standard
mandate — "EVERYTHING MUST BE GOLD STANDARD AND UNIVERSAL"); each CEIR-8 slice = ADR-quality design (0c rigor) →
advisor gate → implementation → tests → 4-config gate, in one slice.

## CEIR-8 — FOUNDATION CLOSURE — ✅ **BAND 8 CLOSED** (8a ✅, 8b ✅, 8c ✅, 8d ✅, 8e ✅, 8f ✅, 8g ✅, 8h ✅, 8i ✅, 8z ✅) (the universality quest, part 1) → U-§112 §121 · mission §10 §99 §101

**Band contract.** Close every U-§112 foundation item BEFORE any further feature band. Universality is achieved by
OPEN-WORLD SEMANTICS (U-§5): a new domain must normally require new dialects/ops/types/attrs/interfaces/providers —
never a second execution architecture (U-§1) — and where a vocabulary stays deliberately closed, the reason is
documented in the ADR. ⛔ NOT a rewrite: the shipped 232-test substrate stays green through every slice; format
changes are versioned recooks. ⛔ The anti-goals (U-§125) bind every design: no universal node enum, no universal
Variant, no Context god-object, no stringly-typed extension surface. **Gate: the U-§121 twenty-item foundation DoD
answered item-by-item from evidence.**

| ✔ | Slice | Deliverable | → §§ |
|---|---|---|---|
| ✅ | CEIR-8a | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-09 (ADR-0111 ACCEPTED).** **The open-world TYPE model — dialect-defined type-classes BESIDE the built-in `TypeKind` fast path, the op model applied to types.** ⭐ **`TypeKind::Extern` is the ONE-TIME enum edit that opens the door — `testcad.toposhape` AND `testeda.net` landed with ZERO further `TypeKind` edits (and every custom type after them, forever).** A `TypeClassId` (interned FNV "dialect.class", mirroring `OpId`) + `Dialect::register_type_class(TypeClassSpec{verify hook, version})` → a `TypeClassInfo` on the Context; an `Extern` `Type` carries `type_class` + `type_class_version` and its PARAMETERS ride the existing slots (`members`/`count`/`cols`/`is_signed`/`fkind`/`name`/`labels`) — so custom types inherit interning, structural equality/hash, arena spans, and serialization FREE (the 3a machinery). ⛔ **The load-bearing invariant:** `operator==` + the intern hasher + `type_is_canonical` all include `type_class`/`type_class_version` (two different classes with identical params = DIFFERENT types; the junk-fields-are-Extern-only canonicality guard closes the prints-identically-but-differs hazard). **String-keyed serialization** (the class STRING via STRP, mirroring op-name encoding) + a **GENERIC canonical text form** (`!extern<CLASS,VER,COUNT,COLS,SIGNED,FKIND,"NAME",NMEM,m..,NLAB,"l"..>` — ⛔ NO pretty print/parse hooks in 8a: they'd emit text an UNREGISTERED Context cannot re-parse, breaking the U-§56 round-trip; named-forward to the language/editor bands). **Binary:** an Extern record's trailing class-string + version, ONLY on `kind==Extern` → **NO version bump** (pre-8a decoders reject the out-of-range kind; pre-8a blobs decode byte-identically — tested). **The verify TRIPLE** (ADR §2.5): the factory `type_extern` ASSERTS, the binary decoder + text parser REJECT (registered-invalid), an UNREGISTERED class PRESERVES opaquely at all three (U-§56). **Version range-check on decode:** a NEWER-schema record than the loader = reject; an older record = accept (migration named-forward — ⚠ **version-sensitivity consequence:** an old-version record is a DISTINCT type from the current-schema type, honest). ⭐ **The U-§56 headline PROVEN both text AND binary:** serialize/print registered → decode/parse into an UNREGISTERED Context → re-serialize/re-print BYTE-EXACT → late-register → the preserved type UNIFIES (same TypeId) with a factory build of the class. Generics: `substitute`/`type_has_params` walk `members` generically — a generic custom type substitutes through, class preserved (tested). Interface hash: the class string emitted CONDITIONALLY (only Extern) → custom types discriminate by class, ZERO churn for existing content. Shape/broadcast queries reach an Extern only by caller error → degrade to Unknown/false via their existing defaults. **Scope fence (named-forward in ADR):** TOML `[[type]]` generator · pretty hooks · attr/data payloads (8b) · migration transforms. **Tests:** `test_extern_type.cpp` (10 `[extern-type]`: dedup + different-class discriminator; the canonicality guard; text+binary round-trip; the U-§56 headline both forms + late-register unify; the verify triple [factory/decoder/parser]; interface-hash class discrimination; version range [newer-reject + older-accept]; substitute-through-Extern; pre-8a no-churn; a single-byte-corruption fuzz sweep over the Extern decode path, ASan-proven). **Gate:** crd-ceir-tests + host + cook **242/242 ctest** (232 + 10) on **win-debug + win-asan + linux-gcc-debug + linux-gcc-asan** (all 3 targets rebuilt fresh against the new `Type` layout — the stale-.obj scar) + LLVM-20 tidy (11 files) + GCC `-Werror=switch` (every `TypeKind` switch carries the Extern arm) + opgen drift/validator (no TOML change) + `crd-ceir-invariants` + invariants. **No binary version bump.** | U-§6 |
| ✅ | CEIR-8b | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-09 (ADR-0112 ACCEPTED).** **The open-world ATTRIBUTE model — TWO wrappers (`TypedConst`, `Extern`) over ONE aggregate vocabulary (`Array`, `Dict`), the 8a type-model surface applied to attributes; the canonicality guard [junk-on-scalar, unsorted/duplicate dict, classless Extern]; nested text+binary round-trip [the child-first pool]; empty aggregates; the U-§56 headline both forms + late-register unify; the verify triple; the wrapper-composition rule; content≠interface hash; version range both forms; a hostile unsorted/duplicate-dict TEXT graceful-reject; the depth guard; pre-8b no-churn; a single-byte-corruption fuzz sweep, ASan-proven). **Gate:** crd-ceir-tests + host + cook **255/255 ctest** (242 + 13) on **win-debug + win-asan + linux-gcc-debug + linux-gcc-asan** (all 3 targets rebuilt fresh against the new `AttrValue` layout — the stale-.obj scar) + LLVM-20 tidy (10 files) + GCC `-Werror=switch` (every `AttrKind` switch carries the four new arms) + opgen drift/validator + `crd-ceir-invariants`. **No binary version bump.** | U-§7 |

| ✅ | CEIR-8d | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-09 (ADR-0114 ACCEPTED).** **Content-independent STABLE SEMANTIC IDENTITY — the ADR-0109 §6 IOU (only SourceLoc landed at 1c).** ⭐ **A `StableId` (u64, 0=invalid) rides `Operation`; ONE id space** — a function IS a `func.func` op, a state slot IS a `StateEdge` op, a visual node's identity IS its op's stable id (no per-kind counters). **Assignment (`Context::assign_stable_ids`) is module-scoped, pre-order, one-time, idempotent** (max+1 for id-0 ops; already-assigned ops never re-derived → survives edits/reorders/renames). ⛔ **NOT a Context counter** (that would break the blob-is-a-pure-function-of-content gate); module-scoped pre-order keeps ids a pure function of content. ⛔ **A monotone WATERMARK** (max id EVER assigned, per module, serialized + restored) — an `erase()`d op is tombstoned/invisible to a live-max scan, so without it a later append would REUSE the dead id and the delete/re-add discriminator would silently pass (state corruption); assignment draws from `max(scan_max, watermark)+1`, so a freed id is never reused (in-memory or across load). **Serialization:** an additive, forward-skippable `STID` chunk (`count + watermark + per-op ids`, body pre-order) → pre-8d decoders skip it, a pre-8d blob decodes fine (ids re-seed pre-order) → ⛔ **NO `kBinaryVersion` bump.** Decode is build-raw-graceful-reject (a 0 id / duplicate / count-mismatch / id>watermark ⇒ reject, never assert; fuzz-swept ASan). ⛔ **The content hash stays id-INDEPENDENT:** `stable_hash` builds the blob WITHOUT the STID chunk → byte-identical to pre-8d → **ZERO content-hash churn, cook-cache hits survive.** The principle: content projections are id-free (`stable_hash` AND the TEXT form — text stays the id-free content projection, documented-why: a per-op id token would churn every print golden for no persistence gain; a test pins that a fresh module's text round-trip reproduces its ids); identity/persistence surfaces (binary STID + the in-memory Module) carry ids. ⭐ **The §20 state schema re-keyed by stable id (sorted, id VALUE in the hash):** a StateEdge-cell REORDER is now invariant (the 7a/8c false-incompatible FIXED), while delete-id-1 + add-id-2 is correctly INCOMPATIBLE (an order-only hash would silently lose state at 10a migration). One shared `assign_stable_ids` used by serialize + interface_hash (no drift). Runtime `m_cells` stays `Operation*`-keyed (transient within-run; cross-version cell matching is 10a). **Interface-hash recook (kCeirCookSchema 2→3); NO binary-format bump.** ⛔ **Unblocks CEIR-10a state migration, CEIR-8i transactions, semantic diff, visual identity; anchors 8c's built-in location kinds' per-instance identity.** **Tests:** `test_stable_id.cpp` (12 `[stable-id]`: pre-order 1..N + idempotent; ids ride the op through serialize; hostile-STID graceful reject; content-hash id-independence; blob purity; the reorder-invariant + delete/re-add-sensitive state schema; the erase-never-reuses-an-id watermark [in-memory + across load]; a genuine pre-8d blob decode→re-serialize fixpoint; `kBinaryVersion==2`; a fresh-module text round-trip reproduces ids; a single-byte fuzz sweep). **Gate:** crd-ceir-tests + host + cook **275/275 ctest** (263 + 12) on **win-debug + win-asan + linux-gcc-debug + linux-gcc-asan** (all 3 targets fresh against the new `Operation`/`Module` layout — the stale-.obj scar) + LLVM-20 tidy (8 files) + GCC + opgen drift/validator + `crd-ceir-invariants`. | U-§101 U-§12 · §10 |

## CEIR-10 — Asset lifecycle completion (band-7 remainder, re-homed) — ✅ **BAND 10 CLOSED 2026-08-10** (10a ✅ / ADR-0120 · 10b ✅ / ADR-0121 · 10z ✅ gate) → §108 §109 §110 §172

**Band contract.** The paused 7c/7d/7z contracts VERBATIM, now consuming their foundation inputs: state migration
keys on **8d stable state identity** (never instance order); the plan cache is an **8h incremental-cache client**;
~~reload transactions ride **8i**~~ → **CORRECTED at 10a (ADR-0120 §2.2):** install atomicity is the 7b `ProgramSlot`
(SET-level all-or-nothing), NOT 8i; 8i is the UPSTREAM authoring seam (an agent edit → 8i commit → content-hash change →
the reload's detect trigger). Reuse pinned at 7b: `ProgramSlot` generations + the interface-hash swap-compat
discriminator (registry drift IS the incompatible case) + the per-generation-Context wholesale-free pattern + the
RAF-11 reentrant-guard scar verbatim.

⭐ **10a DESIGN LOCKED (2026-08-10, 2 advisor consults) → `docs/design/ceir-10a-hot-reload-and-state-migration.md`**
(the ADR-0120 substrate). Key resolutions: migration is a structural DECISION + a live-value MOVE (the 5z
`exec::Interpreter` cells, snapshot/restore-by-8d-stable-id); atomic install is the 7b **SLOT** (SET-level
all-or-nothing), NOT 8i (8i is the upstream authoring seam — a documented in-place correction of "rides 8i");
"compile-affected" = the 8h affected-SET only (compilation named-forward to 11). ⛔ **Cooked format UNTOUCHED — no
schema bump / recook / fuzz** (the host-subset gate compares live-vs-new interface hashes in memory; the expected-callee
`KernelRef` record has no consumer this slice → named-forward to 10b/CKIR-13). Detect front-end **reuses**
`ResourceManager` hot-reload (mtime/debounce/callback), NOT a new file-watch. New: a `contract_hash` (§107 minus the
state schema) so Reject (signature change) is distinguished from Migrate (state-schema-only change).

**10a implementation staged (one row, one DoD — no fragmentation): (1) core exposure · (2) supervisor + set-install ·
(3) migration wiring · (4) detect front-end.** ✅ **Stage 1 DONE + gated (2026-08-10):** the crd-ceir CORE surfaces —
`collect_state_schema` (module-wide `(stable_id, type, depth)` cells) + `contract_hash` (the §107 projection MINUS the
state schema — the Reject-vs-Migrate split) refactored out of `interface_hash` **byte-identically** (the §107 matrix is
the regression net — no recook) + the Interpreter `snapshot_state_by_id`/`restore_state_by_id` cell-value move by 8d
stable id (absent id / depth mismatch → skip → init-fill). 3 stale in-place comment fixes shipped (`program_asset.hpp`
BODY-order→8d + caps-folded-in; `program_asset.hpp` KernelRef CEIR-10→**13**; `runtime_program.hpp` exec-form
CEIR-8→**11**). `test_reload_migration.cpp` (6 `[ceir][reload]`: schema extraction; the 3-way decision table
body/signature/state-only; value migration; absent+depth-mismatch skip). Gate: **364/364 × 4 configs** (win-debug ·
win-asan · linux-gcc-debug · linux-gcc-asan) + LLVM-20 tidy (5 files) + GCC `-Werror=switch` + opgen drift/validator +
`crd-ceir-invariants` (U-§116 pin holds — zero central-enum edits). **NO recook, NO fuzz** (cooked format untouched).
✅ **Stage 2 API-LOCKED (2026-08-10, 3rd advisor consult) → design note §8** (`ReloadSet` in `ceir-cook/hot_reload.{hpp,cpp}`):
heap-`Generation{Context*, RuntimeProgram}` units (the push-back-UAF scar → RuntimeProgram never by-value in a growable
array) + per-Entry ProgramSlot; **the 8h dag keyed `set_revision(asset, content_hash, contract_hash)` IS the validate-set**
(⭐ the advisor's correction: dependent-safety keys on `contract_hash` NOT `interface_hash` — a state-schema-only edit must
NOT reject dependents; `affected_by` = "recompiles-affected" unmodified); a 3-way `ReloadDecision`
{NoChange/HotSwap/NeedsMigration/ContractChange}; **Reject-always on a contract change** (EMPTY≠UNKNOWN — the host is an
unseen caller; leaf-allow named-forward to CEIR-11); reject destroys the candidate Generation (last-good = not installing,
no stored blob); dep graph rebuilt WHOLESALE per membership change (arena-string lifetime); dup-symbol + AssetId-0 loud
reject. ✅ **Stage 2 DONE + gated (2026-08-10):** `ReloadSet` in `ceir-cook/hot_reload.{hpp,cpp}` — heap-`Generation`
units (placement-new Context, reject-destroys-candidate, one-deep zombie drain — ASan-clean); `add`/`reload`/`remove`/
`drain` + `handle`/`is_current`/`program`/`generation`/`affected`; the 8h dag `set_revision(content, contract)` IS the
validate-set (`contract_hash`-keyed dependent-safety); the 3-way `ReloadDecision` (HotSwap installs, the rest keep
last-good); a `Registrar` callback for per-generation dialect registration; dup-symbol + AssetId-0 loud reject.
`test_hot_reload.cpp` (6 `[ceir][reload]`). Gate: **370/370 × 4 configs** + LLVM-20 tidy (3 files) + GCC `-Werror=switch`
(new enums) + opgen + `crd-ceir-invariants` (U-§116 holds — bridge-only, crd-ceir core untouched). **NO recook, NO fuzz**.
✅ **Stage 3 DONE + gated (2026-08-10, advisor-confirmed fork B):** state migration is CALLER-DRIVEN — the ReloadSet
does NOT own interpreters (live §20 cells are populated by EXECUTION, which it never does). A `MigrationFn` (fn-ptr
mutating a `StateSnapshot` array) registered per-asset (`register_migration`, last-wins); its PRESENCE flips a
NeedsMigration reload from Reject → install (the fn runs caller-side, never fake-invoked on an empty snapshot); a free
`migrate_state(old_interp, new_interp, new_module, fn, user)` = snapshot → fn → restore (fn==nullptr = verbatim HotSwap
path). Refusal governs ONLY the cell move (init-fill, no install-rollback). +3 `[ceir][reload]` tests: **the DoD — a real
value survives a depth-1→depth-2 reshape via a mock runtime** (old interp warm=7 → reload installs → migrate_state
expands [7]→[7,7] → new interp continues from 7, control re-inits to 0); refusal→init-fill; HotSwap-verbatim. Gate:
**373/373 × 4 configs** + tidy (3 files) + `-Werror=switch` + opgen + `crd-ceir-invariants` (U-§116 holds — bridge-only).
NO recook, NO fuzz. ✅ **Stage 4 DONE + gated (2026-08-10) — the SOURCE-in detect seam + the RAF-11 reentrant guard
(advisor-corrected scope: not a wrapper watcher, but the honest cook step + bad-edit path stages 1–3 never exercised).**
`add_source`/`reload_source` = a transient cook Context (`cook_program_text`, per-call, §121 text≡builder) → delegate to
`add`/`reload`; a cook failure is a DISTINCT typed outcome (`CookFailed`/`cook_error`) that installs+destroys NOTHING →
**last-good keeps running** (the most common real hot-reload event, finally tested). ⛔ **The RAF-11 reentrant guard**
(`m_reloading` + RAII `GuardScope`): a mutation from inside a registrar/migration fn is a loud typed reject
(`AddError::Reentrant` / `ReloadResult::reentrant`) — proven by a registrar that smuggles the set and re-enters `reload`.
This closes the twice-deferred **7a CookDb/register_cook_handler routing** in its host-subset form; the mtime/filesystem
SIGNAL is production I/O (named-forward — CEIR owns the swap, never the ResourceManager: two-swap-authority). +4
`[ceir][reload]` tests (source hot-swap; **bad source → CookFailed → last-good**; source signature → ContractChange;
reentrant reject). Gate: **377/377 × 4 configs** + tidy (3) + `-Werror=switch` (widened `AddError`) + opgen +
`crd-ceir-invariants` (U-§116 holds — bridge-only). NO recook, NO fuzz. ⭐ **ALL 4 STAGES BUILT — the full 10a lifecycle
works.** ◀ **NEXT = 10a CLOSE** (ADR-0120 describing the settled 4-stage architecture + fork B + the named-forwards; §7
gate-plan reconcile; fresh pre-close advisor; tracker row → ✅).

| ✅ | CEIR-10b | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-10 (ADR-0121).** (was 7d) Execution-plan cache — a `PlanCache` in `ceir-cook/plan_cache.{hpp,cpp}` (bridge-only). ⭐ **A STORE + VALIDATE-ON-HIT client of live truth (advisor fork), NOT a compile pipeline:** the caller compiles an opaque artifact + `put()`s it; `get()` returns hit/miss/stale-deps but ONLY after validating each recorded dep's CURRENT `interface_hash` via a **resolver** fn-ptr → the cache never trusts itself ("caches never truth" made mechanical). ⛔ No compile callback (10b never names the compile→plan interface — deferred to 21/26); deps key on **`interface_hash`** NOT contract (cache-validity wants conservatism — a stale plan is corruption; a spurious recompile is cheap); no second dag (a client of the ReloadSet's 8h dag via the resolver; 0 ⇒ STALE, EMPTY≠UNKNOWN); **in-memory only** (persistence → 11/13 — refines the 10a KernelRef named-forward); bridge-local `PlanDep{AssetId, u64 interface_hash}` (≡ ADR-0109 §85 KernelRef, NOT a crd-ceir mint — I5). Key = {content_hash, target=FNV(provider name), compiler_version}; hit/miss counters first-class (10z asserts them). Tests: `test_plan_cache.cpp` (3 `[ceir][plancache]`: put/get/miss + version + two-targets; §107-extended [callee body-edit→HIT, interface change→StaleDeps, removed→StaleDeps]; clear/evict never-truth) + `test_hot_reload.cpp` +1 (⭐ **the 10z REHEARSAL** — a callee interface change stales exactly `affected(B) ∪ {B}`, C untouched). Gate: **381/381 × 4 configs** + LLVM-20 tidy + opgen + `crd-ceir-invariants` (U-§116 holds). No `plan_status_name` (deliberate — a programmatic result, not a diagnostic surface). **Advisor-reviewed close (ADR-0121 clean; caught 4 design-note doc-vs-reality drifts — reconciled).** ◀ **NEXT = CEIR-10z** (the §172 band-close matrix — a COMPOSING test with hit-count-delta assertions across the 3 edit classes; inherits the pairing rehearsal), which CLOSES BAND 10. | §110 §104 |
| ✅ | CEIR-10z | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-10 → ⭐⭐ BAND 10 CLOSED (10a..10z).** (was 7z) The BAND-10 GATE — the §172 matrix, host subset — a **COMPOSING test** (not doc-synthesis like 9z: band 10's lifecycle + cache guarantees only MEET here, checked in lockstep against a resolver backed by the LIVE ReloadSet). `test_band10_gate.cpp` (1 `[ceir][gate10]`) walks ONE program graph (A imports B; C independent) through the three §172 edit classes: **(1) body-edit B → HotSwap installs live AND caller A's plan stays a HIT** (§107 at the plan layer, proven LIVE — B's own plan self-misses by new content); **(2) signature-edit B → ContractChange REJECT + last-good AND A's plan still HIT** (a reject invalidates nothing); **(3) dep-edit (cold-reload B, new signature) → B's interface changes → A's plan StaleDeps, B self-misses = the recompile set `affected(B) ∪ {B}`, C untouched**. ⛔ **EXACT hit/miss counter deltas asserted at every step** (the 9a not-≥ discipline: 3/0 → 6/1 → 8/1 → 9/3) — the §172 "cache-hit counts". ⛔ Re-verified the 10a/10b public surfaces against code (the 8z discipline); the gate IS the live re-verification. **Advisor pre-close caught 2 (the unasserted ∪{B} half + weak `>`/`≥` counters → exact) — fixed + 4-config re-run.** **Gate: 382/382 ctest × 4 configs** (win-debug + win-asan + linux-gcc-debug + linux-gcc-asan) + LLVM-20 tidy + GCC `-Werror=switch` + opgen + `crd-ceir-invariants` (U-§116 holds). **NO recook, NO fuzz, NO ADR** (a gate composes — the 3z/4z/5z/8z/9z precedent). | §172 |

## CEIR-11 — Reference executor + compiled host plan — ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-10** (11a ✅ / ADR-0122 · 11b ✅ / ADR-0123 · 11c ✅ crd-perf seam+adapter · 11z ✅ §153 audit) — the §84 two-tier execution model COMPLETE: the §118 reference oracle + the compiled tier differential-verified byte-for-byte against it. ⭐ the FIRST fully-closed band of the 2026-08-09 re-baselined spine. → §83 §84 §118 §153

**Band contract.** Two execution tiers (§84). Reference tier: the CEIR-5 executor grown to the full host subset,
maximal diagnostics (the §118 correctness oracle). Compiled tier: `CompiledExecutionPlan` = dense arrays of
pre-resolved op thunks + slot-indexed values — **§153 audited: zero strings, zero per-op heap, zero map lookups,
zero source parsing in the loop** (CountingAllocator-gated, the v14-m allocation-free-infer precedent). Profiler:
every plan op wrapped in `CRD_PERF_SCOPE`-compatible regions (crd-perf, no new profiler). **Gate: no source
parsing / string lookup in the shipping execution loop.**

⭐ **11a DESIGN LOCKED (2026-08-10, advisor consult) → `docs/design/ceir-11a-reference-executor.md`** (the ADR-0122
substrate). Audit (coverage matrix): the executor covers arith(4)/core(10)/func(3)/async-sequential(6); 11a adds the six
§38 residual ops (spawn/continuation/main-thread/worker/fiber-wait/task-group — DON'T EXIST, need TOML+opgen+semantics),
jobs-backed launch/await ON-POOL (today sequential), §112 step hooks (fn-ptr seam, null-default). ⭐ **Value-model risk
CLOSED:** arith is integer-only → the i64 Interpreter covers the full subset (no reshaping fork). Key resolutions:
sequential reference for EVERYTHING lives in **crd-ceir core** (the §118 oracle), jobs-backed in **crd-ceir-host** (rebind
via last-install-wins). Ops-first is MECHANICAL (name-interned → NO format motion/fuzz/recook) — but the six ops'
SIGNATURES are a genuine design sub-fork (§38 is a vocabulary list) → **advisor consult at the stage-2 design point before
authoring TOML**. Internal stages: (1) audit+note ✅ · (2a) the six ops ✅ · (2b-i) §112 hooks + stale
fixes ✅ · (2b-ii-A) pre-flight move to core ✅ · (2b-ii-B) parallel_for/map_reduce sequential reference ✅ · (3)
jobs-backed ON-POOL async ✅ · (4) the 11a DoD gate.

✅ **Stage 3 DONE + gated (2026-08-10) — jobs-backed launch/await ON-POOL via CONDITIONAL POOLING.** ⛔ The crux
(advisor): `async.launch` bodies run IN-FRAME in the reference (captures + state legally work, UNLIKE parallel_for), so
stage 3 must never reject what stage 2 ran. A bridge-local **pool-eligibility classifier** (`exec::region_state_free`
[the exported StateEdge+calls walk] + a NEW no-outer-captures walk) → **pool** (a heap-owned `PooledToken{own scratch,
result, counter}` per launch; the worker runs a fresh sub, fills the result BEFORE the counter decrements = the
happens-before edge; the growable table is heap-owned so it never moves an entry the JobDecl captured by pointer — the
push-back-UAF scar) or **sequential in-frame fallback** (`launch_seq_inframe`); byte-parity by CONSTRUCTION in both
branches. ⛔ **Pooled handles live in a DISJOINT space (`kPoolBase=1<<40`)** so a pooled token never truncates in the
`u32(tok)` cast + aliases a sequential handle; **ALL FIVE consumers** (await/join/race/cancel/fiber_wait) route through
one `resolve_yields`/`token_valid`. spawn/worker → pooled (any thread); main_thread → `pin_thread=0`; group/scope → no
override. `execute()` **drains** the pooled table at exit (leak containment). ⭐ **`pooled_count()` witness (CUMULATIVE —
survives the drain):** asserted ==N pooled / ==0 fallback. Verifies: intern_op read-only on hit; wait pumps thread 0.
`test_host_provider.cpp` +6 (`[ceir][host]` — **T1** N pure launches byte-match the sequential reference + witness==2;
**T2** capturing → fallback, witness==0; **T3** stateful → fallback, witness==0; **T4** main_thread-pinned pooled + awaited
from main completes [the pump path, no deadlock]; **T5** join/race/cancel resolve pooled tokens; **T6** a leaked pooled
token drained at exit [ASan-clean]). Gate: **396/396 × 4 configs** (win-asan + linux-gcc-asan the money configs — the
pooled workers under real threads + the drain, ASan-clean) + tidy (5 files) + GCC `-Werror=switch` + `crd-ceir-invariants`
(crd-ceir core STILL jobs-free — `region_state_free` names no jobs type; the pool is all bridge-side). ⛔ **NO format
motion / fuzz / recook.** ◀ **NEXT = stage 4 (the 11a DoD gate) + ADR-0122** (verify the number then).

✅ **Stage 2b-ii-B DONE + gated (2026-08-10) — the SEQUENTIAL reference for task.parallel_for / task.map_reduce (the
§118 oracle); the "full host subset" reference is COMPLETE.** New file-local `eval_parallel_for_seq`/`eval_map_reduce_seq`
(installed by `install_task_semantics`; the jobs-backed provider OVERRIDES via last-install-wins). ⛔ **The 6z fold
pattern (advisor landmine):** the body runs on ONE fresh SUB-interpreter cloned from `in` (per-index `invoke_region` on
the sub) — NOT `run_region` in `in`'s frame (captures would wrongly resolve) NOR `invoke_region` on the live `in`; runs
the map SEQUENTIALLY in index order → the SAME output as the provider's parallel run. New core surface: an
Interpreter-owned **`map_output` store** (`store_map_output`/`map_output(op)` — the m_yield_store/m_cells family, named
verbatim to mirror `HostProvider::map_output`), `invoke()`/`invoke_region()` capture **`const Module*`** + a `module()`
accessor, a `fuel()` accessor. The provider proto gains `install_task_semantics` (a task.spawn through HostProvider is no
longer NoSemantics). ⛔ **NO execution sharing** — the provider + reference share only the pre-flight ANALYSIS, compute
results INDEPENDENTLY (the bit-exact-blind scar). ⭐ **The first oracle-vs-provider AGREEMENT (the 11b differential
harness in miniature):** `test_host_provider.cpp` +3 (`[ceir][host]`): a non-associative map_reduce (map iv*iv, combine
acc*31+elem) — core-sequential vs HostProvider across **{1..16}** byte-identical (`pin_values`) + `map_output` span-equal;
a stateful body → `ParallelBodyStateful` from the sequential reference (shared pre-flight); an outer-capture body →
`UndefinedValue` in both (the 6z fold pattern's self-contained sub). Gate: **390/390 × 4 configs** (linux-gcc-asan the
money config — the {1..16} threaded run ASan-clean vs the reference) + tidy + GCC `-Werror=switch` + `crd-ceir-invariants`
(crd-ceir STILL jobs-free — the sequential reference names no jobs type). ⛔ **NO format motion / fuzz / recook.** ◀
**NEXT = stage 3 (DESIGN LOCKED 2026-08-10, advisor consult → design note §8; implement next tick):** jobs-backed
launch/await ON-POOL via **CONDITIONAL POOLING** — ⛔ the crux: `async.launch` bodies run IN-FRAME in the reference
(captures + state legally work, UNLIKE parallel_for), so "require pure" would be a capability regression; stage 3 must
never reject what stage 2 ran. A bridge-local **pool-eligibility classifier** (StateEdge-free + calls-resolved + NO outer
captures) → pool (fresh sub, counter = happens-before) or sequential-fallback (bridge-side via run_region/store_yields/
set_value); byte-parity by CONSTRUCTION in both branches. ⛔ Pooled handle > u32-max would TRUNCATE + alias a sequential
handle → ALL FIVE consumers (await/join/race/cancel/fiber_wait) route through one `resolve_token(i64)`. `execute()` DRAINS
the pooled table at exit (leak containment). spawn/worker → pooled; main_thread → pin_thread=0; group/scope → no override.
⭐ **`pooled_count()` witness** (asserted ==N pooled / ==0 fallback — else a never-pools impl passes every parity test).
Verifies DONE: intern_op read-only on hit (no worker-read race); wait pumps thread 0 (execute() must run on main). T1–T6.
Then stage 4 (11a DoD gate) + ADR-0122.

✅ **Stage 2b-ii-A DONE + gated (2026-08-10) — the parallel-purity pre-flight MOVED to crd-ceir core** (the 9d
hoist-at-second-consumer; zero behavior change). `PfResult`/`preflight_region`/`check_parallel_region`/`preflight` (the
StateEdge-free + arity + terminator analysis, PURE IR — no jobs types) lifted from the crd-ceir-host bridge's anon
namespace to public `exec::` surface (`exec::PreflightResult` · `exec::check_parallel_region` · `exec::preflight_parallel`);
the provider's `execute()` now DELEGATES. ⭐ **So the provider's PARALLEL run + the (2b-ii-B) reference's SEQUENTIAL run
share ONE legality analysis — agree by construction.** Swapped the bridge-era `"func.call"`/`"task.*"` STRING compares for
interned OpIds (idempotent intern → non-const `Context&`). The conservative module-wide over-rejection is documented as an
oracle/provider divergence (not a bug). Gate: **387/387 × 4 configs** (core + bridge relink) + tidy + GCC `-Werror=switch`
+ `crd-ceir-invariants` (crd-ceir STILL jobs-free — I4 holds, the pre-flight names no jobs type). ⛔ **NO format motion / no
new tests** (a behavior-preserving move — the existing host-provider parallel tests exercise it via delegation). ◀ **NEXT
= stage 2b-ii-B** (the sequential reference EvalFns via the 6z fold pattern [fresh sub-interpreter, per-index invoke_region;
NOT run_region-in-frame — captures wrongly resolve — NOR invoke_region-on-`in` — live frame] + an Interpreter-owned
`map_output` store [the m_yield_store/m_cells family] + `invoke()` captures `const Module*` + a `fuel()` accessor + the
first oracle-vs-provider agreement tests; ⛔ NO execution sharing — the bit-exact-blind scar).

✅ **Stage 2b-i DONE + gated (2026-08-10) — §112 step hooks + 3 stale-comment fixes** (split from 2b: core-only, no
bridge relink — distinct blast radius from 2b-ii's pre-flight move). The §112 debugger SEAM (hooks only): a pre-op + a
post-op `StepHook = void(*)(const Operation&, void*)` + `set_step_hooks(pre, post, user)` on the Interpreter — pre fires
BEFORE each dispatched op, post ONLY after a SUCCESSFUL dispatch; ⛔ null-default (zero work when unset — one null check
in `eval_op`); NOT proto-copied (per-session, like set_user/cancel). 3 stale-comment fixes: `exec.hpp` "per-width typing
joins CEIR-6" → integer-only-through-11a + "Jobs-backed parallel async CEIR-6c" → CEIR-11a; `task.ceirop.toml` header
"route to CEIR-8" → LANDED at CEIR-11a. `test_task.cpp` +1 (`[ceir][task]`: exact pre/post counts [2/2 — const+return, the
not-≥ discipline] + null-default zero-cost). Gate: **387/387 × 4 configs** + tidy + GCC `-Werror=switch` + opgen
drift-clean (TOML comment) + `crd-ceir-invariants` (U-§116). ⛔ **NO format motion / fuzz / recook.** ◀ **NEXT = stage
2b-ii** (the parallel_for/map_reduce sequential reference — move `check_parallel_region` from the bridge to crd-ceir core,
the 9d hoist-at-2nd-consumer precedent so oracle+provider agree by construction; relinks the bridge).

✅ **Stage 2a DONE + gated (2026-08-10) — the six §38 residual ops (advisor-designed signatures).** `task.spawn`/
`main_thread`/`worker` `{body}→%token` (TokenProducer, share `eval_launch` — placement value-unobservable in the §118
oracle) · `task.continuation` `(%tok){body(%vals)}→%next` (Consumer+Producer, NEW `eval_continuation`: antecedent yields
→ body block-args → new token) · `task.fiber_wait` `(%tok)→(%vals)` (mirrors `eval_await`, host wait yields the fiber) ·
`task.group` `{body}→(%results)` (NO token traits — bounds not forks, shares `eval_scope`, confinement layered on 5b+6a).
Layering invariant: async = concurrency STRUCTURE (no execution commitment) · task = host/job PLACEMENT (the shared-EvalFn
model is the state/delay/history precedent). ⛔ **Blocking pre-TOML verify PASSED** — crd-jobs offers every lowering
(run/wait/pin_thread=0/parallel_for) → no cook-only-gate. New `install_task_semantics`. ⭐ **The open-world payoff
ASSERTED:** the 6a `find_token_misuse` verifier + the generated smoke tests cover all six ops with **ZERO verifier edits**;
the §32 audio-RT flip fires on a task op. `test_task.cpp` (4 `[ceir][task]`: spawn→fiber_wait roundtrip · continuation
chain (10+5=15) · Unconsumed + double-consume · audio flip) + 3 auto-grown generated smoke. Gate: **386/386 × 4 configs**
+ tidy + GCC `-Werror=switch` + opgen drift-clean (regen) + `crd-ceir-invariants` (U-§116). ⛔ **NO format motion / fuzz /
recook** (name-interned — the CEIR-2 doctrine held). ◀ **NEXT = stage 2b.**

BAND 11 OPENS.** Reference executor grown to the FULL host subset (the §118 oracle for the 11b compiled tier) + step hooks + the inbound band-6 async work — shipped as 4 gated stages (design note `ceir-11a-reference-executor.md`; Tests: `test_task.cpp` (6 `[ceir][task]`) + `test_host_provider.cpp` (agreement + 6 ON-POOL `[ceir][host]`). **Gate: 397/397 × 4 configs** (win-asan+linux-gcc-asan the threaded money configs) + tidy + GCC `-Werror=switch` + opgen + `crd-ceir-invariants`. **NO recook, NO fuzz, NO binary version bump.** | §118 §84 §112 §38 |
| ✅ | CEIR-11b | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-10 (ADR-0123) — the COMPILED tier of §84, differential-verified byte-for-byte against the §118 reference oracle across a program corpus.** DESIGN LOCKED 2026-08-10 (advisor consult → `docs/design/ceir-11b-compiled-execution-plan.md`, the ADR-0123 substrate). Plan compiler + `CompiledExecutionPlan` + the differential harness (reference vs compiled, `pin_values` byte-compare over the corpus). ⭐ **The representation (advisor-corrected): STRUCTURED CONTROL FLOW COMPILED TO DENSE INSTRUCTION ARRAYS** — the Module is compile-time INPUT ONLY; the plan owns a tree of dense `Instr{thunk, operand-slot span, result-slot span, immediate, child-seq indices}`; ⛔ the hot loop NEVER touches an `Operation*` (a structural Region walk = "authoring-graph traversal", still a §153 violation; flat jump-streams rejected — §153 wants DENSITY not flatness). Slots: every SSA Value → dense u32 slot, flat i64 store per frame (kills the env map); thunks pre-resolved (kills the dispatch map). ⛔ **The three HIDDEN §153 violations (the real 11z risks, VERIFIED):** per-eval attr reads (`eval_const` "value", `eval_cmpi` "predicate" STRING, state "depth") → compile-time immediates/enums; the state-cell `HashMap<Operation*,Cell>` → dense cell indices + pre-baked per-block LATCH LISTS; `resolve_call` per call → compiled-function index + arena frame windows. ⛔ **Compiled thunks are INDEPENDENT impls** (never delegate to the reference EvalFns — the bit-exact-blind scar; share the SPEC not code). Compile-time rejection = a typed `CompileError` (the differential covers programs that compile). Home = crd-ceir core `plan.{hpp,cpp}`; compiles the SEQUENTIAL semantics; ⛔ does NOT name the §69 compile→plan interface (→ 21/26). **PlanCache: the plan is an IN-MEMORY object** (raw thunk ptrs non-serializable) — NOT wedged into 10b's byte-artifact; the serializable blueprint (thunk-TABLE indices) is named-forward. Corpus (⛔ "7z" is pre-renumber → band-7's §121 cooked-vs-builder-twin byte-identity AT THE PLAN LAYER): the 5z gate (loop/switch/calls/state) + the 6z map-reduce + exec/async/task, via a SHARED BUILDER header (rich_graph.hpp/1f precedent). Stages: (1) note ✅ · (2) straight-line arith ✅ · (3a) control flow ✅ · (3b) §20 state · (4) calls + async/task · (5) full-corpus differential + ADR-0123 + close. Advisor on each stage's design. ✅ **Stage 2 DONE + gated (2026-08-10):** `plan.{hpp,cpp}` (crd-ceir core) — `compile(ctx, module, entry)` → a dense `CompiledPlan{instrs, operand/result pools, num_slots, param/result slots}` for straight-line arith (const/addi/muli/cmpi + func.return); the compile-time slot allocator (a `Value*→u32` map — COMPILE-time only) + the §153 immediates (const value, the cmpi "predicate" STRING → a dense `CmpPred` enum ONCE); `run(plan, args)` — a flat i64 slot array, a **dense switch** dispatch (§153: a jump table, NOT a map/string/heap touch, NO `Operation*`). ⛔ INDEPENDENT thunks (never call the reference EvalFns). Typed `CompileError` for not-yet-supported ops (a legit tier difference — the differential covers programs that compile). ⭐ **The DIFFERENTIAL harness skeleton:** `test_plan.cpp` (3 `[ceir][plan]`) — a `differential()` helper runs @entry through BOTH the reference interpreter AND the compiled plan, `pin_values` byte-compares (straight-line arith 5*5+3=28; cmpi sgt both ways; UnsupportedOp/NoEntry rejects). Gate: **400/400 × 4 configs** + tidy + GCC `-Werror=switch` + `crd-ceir-invariants` (crd-ceir jobs-free — the plan is pure). NO recook/fuzz/version-bump. ✅ **Stage 3a DONE + gated (2026-08-10) — CONTROL FLOW compiled to CHILD instruction arrays** (split from stage 3: control flow now, §20 state → 3b). The plan is now a TREE of dense `Seq{instrs, yield_slots}` (each Seq owns its instrs — nested but movable, no interleaving); a control-flow `Instr` records CHILD SEQ indices (into `child_pool`) + the recursive compiler (`compile_seq`) compiles each region's block → a child seq; ⛔ the executor `run_seq` recurses into child seqs BY INDEX (never an `Operation*`/Region). `If`/`Scope` forward the child's yield → results; `For` binds the induction slot (imm) + loops [lo,hi) step (⛔ `RunError::BadForStep` on step≤0); `While` = cond-yields-1 loop (`CondArity`); `Switch`/`Match` = run `children[sel]` (`SelectorOutOfRange`). ⭐ **New `RunError`/`RunResult`** — the differential compares the ERROR too (a runtime BadForStep/SelectorOutOfRange must agree). ## CEIR-13 — Compute + transfer (first GPU contact) — ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-10** (13z ✅ the §129 execution proof + the ADR-0108 cornerstone flip executed; **BAND-13 DONE — NEXT = CEIR-14**) (13a ✅ + 13b ✅ + 13c ✅ + 13d ✅ CLOSED 2026-08-10 — the ceir.compute dispatch dialect [13a], the ceir.transfer movement dialect [13b], CKIR-by-identity: the [op.kernel_ref] machinery + DependencyRecord.ckir_refs + the cook's KernelResolveFn interface-hash contract check [13c, CDEP v5]; the LOWERING pass in the NEW `crd-ceir-gpu` BRIDGE [13d: 1a scaffold + ADR-0125 · 1b dispatch lowering · 2 transfer + ambient-narrowing (precise per-binding barriers) · 3 the 12c view→hazard retrofit + FLIP — all DONE, 481/481×4], §158/§79; NEXT = 13z the §129 EXECUTION PROOF [add/reduce/scan/FFT as CEIR assets run through the lowering, Vulkan+DX12 — ADR-0108 cornerstone flip] and any 13e) → §42 §50 §85 · PROOF §129

**Band contract.** `ceir.compute` (§42) + `ceir.transfer` (§50), lowered onto the EXISTING canonical command model
(`gpu-context` `command_model.hpp` / `detail/command_lowering.hpp`) and `IComputeContext` **via the `crd-ceir-gpu`
bridge module** (ADR-0109 §4.2 — the render/compute provider; `crd-ceir` core never links gpu-context) — CEIR
orchestrates the proven layer, it does not invent GPU plumbing (§158). CKIR by identity (§85): `compute.dispatch @kernel` references
CKIR programs by content-hash; compiler-EMITTED CKIR deferred to CEIR-26. **The proof reuses already-bit-exact
kernels** (`build_reduce`, `build_scan`, radix sort, FFT) — the new thing under test is ORCHESTRATION, not math.
**Scars:** upload→first-read barrier (grid-size time bomb) · dispatch_1wg · timing asserts same-pass-only.
**Proof (§129):** add/reduction/scan/FFT program asset on both backends.
