## CEIR-15 — FrameGraph unification — ✅ **CLOSED 2026-08-11** (all 15a–15z ✅ 4-config; the FrameGraph is a CEIR dialect gated by validate_ceir_frame, old path deleted §121, ADR-0106 amended) (decision packet `docs/design/ceir-15-framegraph-unification.md`; advisor-consulted; sources read: master roadmap §39 [ceir.frame owns the full topology model] + §126 [8 migration steps = 15a–15z 1:1], ADR-0106 [superseded @ 15f — frontends survive], ADR-0109 §4.2 [crd-ceir host-only ⇒ crd-frame-cook→crd-ceir is acyclic]). ⭐ **The frame asset is ALREADY CEIR-shaped** (the RAF-12.3 §7 fold: a pass = executor-id + reads/writes + typed param bag = identity + operands + attrs) — CEIR-15 is a PROMOTION, not a rewrite. **Dialect LOCKED (FOUR ops, advisor-refined 2026-08-11): `frame.graph` region + `frame.pass` + `frame.draw_list` + `frame.history`; RESOURCES REUSE `resource.declare`/`import`** (NOT a new frame.resource op — the 12b/c/d planner is built over declare's attrs; the 11 FrameResourceKinds decompose onto lifetime={transient|persistent|history} + import + frame-scoped OPEN attrs; PingPong = lifetime=history,history_length=1). `frame.pass`: executor = a SYMBOL attr (ExecutorTypeId derived by hash — never one-op-per-executor); ⛔ reads/writes = ONE variadic `resources` tail + an `access` {r|w|rw} token string (opgen: only the last operand may be variadic — the compute.dispatch/render.draw pattern); resources-as-operands ⇒ CEIR's hazard/lifetime machinery DERIVES the barriers (the §159 realization). `frame.history(R)→underlying-type` = the Fork B read op (B1, RESOLVED): resource_root chases only resource.view by op kind, so a distinct op's result is its own root ⇒ the false-intra-frame-RAW fix is STRUCTURAL. **Checks ran:** AttrKind has NO Vec4 (decompose, 14a precedent); CEIR attrs are OPEN (opgen verifies only required) ⇒ the param bag maps to attrs directly, app params validated at cook against the EXECUTOR schema. References = OPTIONAL SYMBOL attrs on frame.pass (⛔ NOT kernel_ref — executor is a MECHANIC name, no interface hash; kernel_ref is one-per-op but a pass refs shader+kernel+technique+6 RT programs ⇒ the multi-ref CDEP extraction is NAMED to 15c, not the 15a toml). Migration = a CONVERTER PAIR FrameGraphDesc↔ceir.frame with a ROUND-TRIP-IDENTITY gate (backward converter ⇒ 15e incremental). **Fork B (history/ping-pong hazard) ✅ RESOLVED = B1** (frame.history read op, structural via resource_root by-op-kind). Births **ADR-0127** (ceir.frame + converter placement F1 = in crd-frame-cook). **15a-1 ✅ dialect COMPILES + 15a-2 ✅ DONE + gated 2026-08-11** — `frame.ceirop.toml` (4 ops: frame.graph/pass/draw_list/history), opgen (drift-clean, validator 59/59), `frame.hpp`/`frame.cpp` (register_dialect + `draw_list` marker type-class); **`Context::find_frame_misuse`** (the find_render_misuse mirror, `scan_frame_region` — 13 kinds: PassOutsideGraph/ForeignCommandInGraph/ExecutorNotSymbol/PassAccess{Invalid,Arity}/PassOperandNotResource/ForEach/Queue/DrawListCull/Sort/Limit/HistoryOperandNotHistory/HistoryFramesBackInvalid; reuses ceir_render_string_ok/ceir_parse_access/ceir_is_resource_kind/ceir_op_is_gpu_command; the frame.history operand's lifetime=history-declare identity via defining_op). `test_frame.cpp` (positive + §121-text round-trip + 11-section negative matrix). ⛔ CAUGHT: the tests/ceir CMakeLists lists sources EXPLICITLY (not glob) — test_frame.cpp was silently NOT compiled (only the opgen-generated frame SMOKE tests rode in via ${CEIR_GENERATED_TESTS}); the first "gate" ran only smoke tests. Fixed (added test_frame.cpp) — the exe now lists the 3 real tests (ctest #458-460). Gate (4 configs, REAL tests): win-debug/asan full ceir 432/432 (3272 assertions) + [frame] 6/6 (45 assertions); linux-gcc-debug/asan 432/432 (gcc -Werror=switch clean, LSan-clean); tidy(5). **15a-3a ✅ DONE + gated 2026-08-11 — the FORWARD converter FrameGraphDesc→ceir.frame + ADR-0127.** ⭐ **ADR-0127 ACCEPTED** (the ceir.frame dialect + the converter placement): the converter lives IN crd-frame-cook (a new `crd-frame-cook → crd-ceir` link edge — acyclic by construction, crd-ceir is host-only per ADR-0109 §4.2; NO bridge needed for a host-to-host cook converter). `frame_ceir.{hpp,cpp}` `to_ceir_frame(desc, ctx)→Module`: resources→resource.declare/import (kind→lifetime {transient|persistent|history}/import + a `name` attr for asset-id/round-trip; PingPong→history+history_length=1), draw_lists→frame.draw_list (all/any/none comma-joined + cull/sort/limit), passes→frame.pass (executor NAME as a symbol — a 14-builtin id→name table + the custom p.executor; reads/writes resolved by a NAME→Value map → the `resources` operand tail + `access` w/r tokens; the kDrawList param → a draw_list operand; for_each/queue + shader/kernel/technique ref params → symbol attrs). `test_frame_ceir.cpp` (a FrameGraphBuilder scene → to_ceir_frame → find_frame_misuse CLEAN + the ceir text round-trips print==parse-print). ⛔ re-hit last tick's lesson: tests/frame-cook CMakeLists is EXPLICIT — added test_frame_ceir.cpp (verified --list-tests shows the 2 cases). Gate (4 configs): win-debug/asan [ceir][frame] 2/2 (7 assertions) + full crd-frame-cook 27/27 (681); linux-gcc-debug/asan same (gcc = the new edge acyclic, LSan-clean); tidy(3). **15a-3b-1 ◧ forward-complete 2026-08-11 (win-debug verified):** the forward converter now stores the FULL round-trip field set so the backward can recover a byte-identical desc — added `name` attrs to frame.pass + frame.draw_list (toml + opgen regen, drift-clean) for pass/draw-list name recovery; the resource.declare/import now carries `frame_kind` (the EXACT FrameResourceKind as its enum int — lifetime alone is lossy) + `format` (the FgImageFormat enum int — no format-string mapping to expose; emit reads the reconstructed enum) + width/height/scale/layers/samples/mips/sampled/storage/depth_buffer/no_alias/resizable/stride/count/size_bytes (non-defaults only). **15a-3b ✅ DONE + gated 2026-08-11 — the converter PAIR is LOSSLESS (the round-trip-identity gate is GREEN).** `from_ceir_frame(ctx, m, alloc, out)` reconstructs the FrameGraphDesc from the ceir.frame module: name/schema from the frame.graph attrs; resources from the resource.declare/import attrs (name/frame_kind/format/sizing/flags); draw_lists from name/all-any-none-split/cull/sort/limit; passes from name/executor (→executor_id via crd::renderpass::executor_type_id, +p.executor iff !is_builtin) + reads/writes recovered from the `resources` operands' `access` tokens + the operands' `name` attrs (a frame.draw_list operand → the kDrawList param) + for_each/queue/shader/kernel/technique. ⭐ **THE ROUND-TRIP-IDENTITY GATE**: `emit_frame_toml(desc) == emit_frame_toml(to→from(desc))` — a FrameGraphBuilder scene survives desc→ceir→desc′ BYTE-IDENTICALLY (FrameGraphDesc has no operator==; the emitted TOML is its canonical form, the §121 precedent). ⛔ **CAUGHT+FIXED a generalizable CEIR-reader bug**: `attr_value(an INVALID AttrId)` returns a default AttrValue (kind=Int, i=0), so a naive `kind==Int?i:def` reads an ABSENT attr as 0, not the caller's default — layers/mips/samples round-tripped 1→0. Every attr reader now checks `op->attr(name).valid()` first (banked as a scar; a round-trip gate is what catches this class — a forward-only test never exercises "absent⇒default"). Gate (4 configs): win-debug/asan [ceir][frame] 3/3 (10 assertions) + full crd-frame-cook 28/28 (684) + ceir [frame] 6/6 (45); linux-gcc-debug/asan same (gcc, LSan-clean); tidy(3). ⭐ **15a-3 (converter PAIR) + 15a + 15b all COMPLETE — both §126 frontends (.frame.toml @ 15a-3a, FrameGraphBuilder @ 15b) END at byte-identical ceir.frame. NEXT = 15c (the cook VALIDATIONS → CEIR verifiers: the 38-D4-class varying-contract/header-word/shape checks + the executor-schema param validation become CEIR verifiers, each a POINTING diagnostic — §126 step 3), DEVICE-FREE.** → §39 §126 §159

**Band contract.** The §126 eight steps, seam by seam. `frame-cook`'s `FrameGraphDesc` parse emits `ceir.frame`
(§39) instead of the private blob; `FrameGraphBuilder` emits the same; the 38-D4-class cook validations become CEIR
verifiers; `FrameGraphTemplate`/`CompiledFrameGraph` (ADR-0106) become the lowering/plan layer; `execute_frame`
runs CEIR plans. **Invariant preserved:** `frame-cook ⊥ crd-scene` via the `IFrameGraphHost` seam (host resolves
ECS→pre-resolved `DrawItem`; CEIR never sees a scene type). **Per-asset parity gate:** every shipped built-in frame
asset (`forward_csm`, deferred, the sandbox frames) renders PIXEL-IDENTICAL through CEIR before its old path dies —
with a deterministic clock (the A/B-needs-deterministic-clock scar). **ADR-0106 struck in place @ 15f.** Asset IDs
preserved. FrameGraph is not deprecated syntax — its semantics (topology/lifetime/history) become a CEIR dialect
(§159).

| ✔ | Slice | Deliverable | → §§ |
|---|---|---|---|
| ✅ | CEIR-15a | **✅ DONE + gated 2026-08-11.** The `ceir.frame` dialect (`frame.graph`/`frame.pass`/`frame.draw_list`/`frame.history`; resources REUSE `resource.declare`/`import`) + the `FrameGraphDesc ↔ ceir.frame` converter PAIR + the round-trip-identity gate (ADR-0127). 15a-1 dialect (toml+opgen+type-class+register) → 15a-2 `find_frame_misuse` (13 `FrameMisuseKind`s) + structural/§121-text tests → 15a-3 the converter pair (`to_ceir_frame`/`from_ceir_frame`) + `emit_frame_toml(desc)==emit_frame_toml(to→from(desc))` gate. DEVICE-FREE, acyclic `crd-frame-cook → crd-ceir`. | §39 §126 |
| ✅ | CEIR-15b | **✅ DONE + gated 2026-08-11.** `FrameGraphBuilder` → same canonical `ceir.frame` as the `.frame.toml` frontend (§126 step 2). Since `to_ceir_frame` is DETERMINISTIC from a FrameGraphDesc and emit→parse is a lossless desc round-trip (the REN-36 gate), a builder-built graph and the SAME graph parsed from its emitted TOML lower to BYTE-IDENTICAL ceir.frame — the "one runtime/compiler, no privileged frontend" contract (§39). `test_frame_ceir.cpp` "ceir 15b" (build via FrameGraphBuilder → path1 = to_ceir_frame(desc), path2 = to_ceir_frame(parse(emit(desc))); assert print(m1)==print(m2)). ⭐ needed a VALID graph (parse_frame_toml VALIDATES, unlike the emit-only round-trip) → build_scene now writes `@output` (PresentSourceInternal caught the old present-reads-a-transient); ADDED `@output` IMPLICIT-EXTERNAL handling to the converter (to_ceir_frame mints a resource.import — NO frame_kind attr — for any pass reference not declared; from_ceir_frame SKIPS an import without frame_kind; the §39 output endpoint). ⛔ the @output import name is a STRING attr not a symbol — a CEIR symbol can't round-trip '@' through text. Gate (4 configs): win-debug/asan [ceir][frame] 4/4 (14 assertions) + full crd-frame-cook 29/29 (688); linux-gcc-debug/asan same + [frame] 6/6; tidy(2). | §126 §121 |
| ✅ | CEIR-15c | **✅ 15c CLOSED + row RE-VERIFIED 2026-08-11 (GATE-re-verifies-rows doctrine: crd-frame-cook 92/92 assertions 2661, 4-config this session — validate_ceir_frame is the full semantic verifier; converter lossless over its flattened-desc input domain).** Cook validations → CEIR verifiers (varying-contract, header-word, shape — each a pointing diagnostic). **15c-0 ✅ DONE + gated 2026-08-11: the `frame.history` ROUTING fix (Fork-B B1) + the ping-pong round-trip LOCK.** `to_ceir_frame` now routes a pass READ of a `lifetime=history` (PingPongImage) resource through a `frame.history` op (a distinct `resource_root` — no false intra-frame RAW vs the same-frame write); `from_ceir_frame` recovers the read name from the wrapped declare. A TAA-history fixture (`build_taa`, the builder makes only transients so the PingPong is pushed directly) locks it: `find_frame_misuse`-clean, a `frame.history` op is present, and the desc round-trips `emit_frame_toml`-identical. `test_frame_ceir.cpp` "ceir 15c" (#3950; advisor-hardened — the fixture is now `fb.validate()==Ok`, absolute-sized PingPong dodging `PersistentNeedsSize`, a draw-list dodging `MissingDrawList`, and a `frame.history` TEXT parse→print fixpoint — the op had never been text-round-tripped). Gate: win-debug/asan [ceir 15] 8/8 + linux-gcc-debug [ceir 15] 5/5 + clang-tidy(win-tidy) `frame_ceir.cpp` clean. **The §115 classification table (every `FrameCookError` → PARSER \| STRUCTURAL-IMPOSSIBLE \| CEIR-ANALYSIS(15d) \| FRAME-VERIFIER \| PROGRAM-CONTRACT + the 6 NEW-IN-CEIR guards) is in `docs/design/ceir-15-framegraph-unification.md` — the 15c-1 driver.** **15c-1a ✅ DONE + gated 2026-08-11: the 4 NEW-IN-CEIR structural guards** (all executor-agnostic ⇒ crd-ceir CORE `find_frame_misuse` kinds, appended at enum end): `OperandOutsideGraph` (a pass/history operand defined outside this frame.graph region — a block-arg or foreign region; `def->parent_block()->parent_region()==r`), `MultipleGraphs` (a module-level pre-order `second_frame_graph` walk, nested graphs included — a FrameGraphDesc is exactly one), `HistoryReadNotThroughFrameHistory` (a pass READS a lifetime=history declare directly — the Fork-B B1 STRUCTURAL teeth), `HistoryWriteThroughHistory` (advisor-caught inverse: a pass WRITES a frame.history result). ## ⛔⛔⛔ BAND-18 CORRECTION — "authored asset" INCLUDES the PROGRAMS, not just the frame graph (user 2026-08-15, in anger)

**The scar.** Under CEIR-18a-1 I authored the Forward+ light cull as `build_cluster_light_cull` — a hand-written C++
`KGraph` builder in `engine/kir/include/crd/kir/ckir_light_cull.hpp`, with NO disk declaration and NO disk-first cook.
The user caught it and ELEVATED the band's bar: **every rendering/compute ALGORITHM a pass runs must ALSO be an
authorable asset program** — cooked disk-first, default-loaded for every app, partially modifiable or completely
replaceable by an app asset — NOT a hand-built `ensure_*_program`/`ensure_*_kernel` registered from C++. (AGENTS.md
§Agent Conduct "THE SAME LAW BINDS THE PROGRAMS"; docs/PRINCIPLES.md; memory `feedback_everything_is_an_authorable_asset_ceir`.)

**What the band as-written permitted (and must no longer).** The CEIR-18 row treats "renderer as authored asset" = the
`.frame.toml` is authored; shader programs referenced by id may be host-registered via `register_raster`/`register_kernel`
with a HAND-BUILT graph. CEIR-18c shipped exactly this: `deferred.frame.toml` is authored + correct, but the
`engine://scene/deferred_lighting` program behind it is hand-built C++ (`ensure_deferred_lighting_program`,
scene_renderer.cpp:2180). The frame-asset + device gates are GREEN and STAY; the PROGRAM's provenance is the re-opened debt.

**The elevated bar (binding for all remaining 18 work + a re-open sweep):** a renderer slice is DONE only when BOTH its
frame graph AND every program it runs are authorable assets cooked disk-first, and the hand-built C++ builder is DELETED.
Test before done: can an app replace the algorithm by editing/shadowing an asset, WITHOUT recompiling the engine?

⛔⛔⛔ **LOAD CONVENTION (user correction 2026-08-15, in anger — I had loaded every `.ckir` via the WRONG seam):** an authored
program asset is loaded through the **RAF-9/10 canonical-id convention**, APP-FIRST, so an app can REPLACE it with no recompile —
NOT bare `asset_text` (Engine-mount-only, an app's mount is unreachable) and NEVER `ifstream`. `.ckir` is now a first-class asset
TYPE (`render-asset-core/identity.cpp`: `infer_type("ckir")=Program`, `asset_extension("ckir")=".ckir"`); the files live at
`assets/ckir/<name>.ckir`; the engine loads via `Impl::resolve_program_text("ckir/<name>")` = try `app://ckir/<name>` (app file
WINS), else `engine://ckir/<name>`. Later `.chir` gets its own folder the same way. Memory:
`feedback_authored_programs_load_via_resolve_program_text_engine_app_convention`. ⛔ 18a-2's cull wiring MUST use this seam too.

**The MAP — hand-built C++ program/kernel builders in `scene_renderer.cpp` (provisional, by cook-vs-`create_program`
signature; confirm each at conversion):**
- FRAGMENT (fullscreen ALGORITHM → asset): ~~`ensure_deferred_lighting_program`~~ ✅ (18c, `deferred_lighting.ckir`) ·
  ~~`ensure_taa_program`~~ ✅ (18p, `taa_resolve.ckir` — load-time lower + runtime NDC±Y sign) · ~~`ensure_hzb_program`~~ ✅
  (18p, `hzb_build.ckir` — param-free, exercises `tex_gather`+`Min`; library `body_hzb_build` STAYS as oracle) ·
  ~~`ensure_moment_program`~~ ✅ CONVERTED 2026-08-15 (18p — 3 assets + spec-const params, REN-40-D 43/2 BOTH backends via
  asset-load; advisor-LOCKED: **LOAD-TIME PARAM SPECIALIZATION** — the asset carries the ALGORITHM,
  host patches `layer`/direction constant-NODES per variant before `create_program` into the existing `moment_prog[kind][index]`
  slots; `inv_size` DERIVED in-graph via **`TexSize`** [op EXISTS] so it's NOT a param; assets = `moment_convert_evsm.ckir` +
  `moment_convert_msm.ckir` + `moment_blur.ckir`. ⛔ bake-all is a §128 KILL [`inv_size`=live 1/map_size]; TAA-buffer is dead
  [`layer` varies per-pass-per-frame]. ✅ D12 CHECK DONE (2026-08-15): params = D12 **SPEC-CONST nodes** (`g.spec_constant`);
  `axes`+`iidx` round-trip FREE (default-elided) ⇒ **NO `[[param]]` table / form change**; both backends use the node `cval`
  (Vulkan `constant_id` default / DX12 baked literal — gpu-context has NO `VkSpecializationInfo`), so the host patches `cval`
  via the new **`KGraph::set_spec_const(id,val)`** after `ckir_read` + before `create_program`. **STEP1+2 ✅ DONE (2026-08-15):**
  `body_moment_convert`(layer→spec) + `body_moment_blur`(layer/dir/**inv**→spec); ⛔ inv is a SPEC-CONST fed from live
  `csm.map_size`, NOT a TexSize derive (the arrayed atlas `textureSize` is ivec3 but `KOp::TexSize` types a Tex2D as ivec2 —
  a rank miscompile on materialization); caller UNCHANGED (spec defaults = passed values ⇒ bit-identical bake). REN-40-D
  **43/2 BOTH backends** (EVSM+MSM soft shadows unchanged). 3 assets emitted (`moment_convert_evsm`/`_msm` + `moment_blur`) +
  kir `[ckir18q]`=**246/8** (round-trip + NON-VACUITY pin: `set_spec_const` 2 inv values ⇒ graphs DIFFER). **STEP3-5 ✅ DONE:**
  `ensure_moment_program` is a THIN per-kind asset-load (convert reads evsm/msm by soft_mode; blur reads `moment_blur`;
  `set_spec_const(layer=index)` + blur `(dir, inv=1/LIVE-map_size)`; create into `moment_prog[kind][index]`); the
  `body_moment_*` build calls are DELETED (bodies STAY in ckir_technique.hpp as oracle/regen source). **REN-40-D 43/2 BOTH
